# Findings: Reach, mutation and disposition for the 9qvfie rollup outcomes
## Inputs

- Measured HEAD: `86f0d88d9e967a79af6b3e6cd335081393fdbf72`
- Candidate counts from `hpjj44`: 747 `production-key` rows and 17 `test-file` rows (total 764 candidate items).
- Extraction command:
  ```sh
  python3 .aw/state/trimcarry-8dyt88/extract_inputs.py
  ```
  Extracts `keys.txt` and `files.txt` from `.aw/records/research/20261013-uncitedaudit-01-hpjj44-uncited-trim-audit.findings.md` section `## Triaged not mutated`.
- Discrepancy note on pass-shared names: The backlog item `9qvfie` body listed 17 `pass-shared` file names, but 12 of those names (e.g. `tests/test_cli_commands.py`) never existed in git history (`git log --all -- tests/<name>` is empty). As backlog `9qvfie` notes ("details in hpjj44 ## Triaged not mutated"), `hpjj44` `## Triaged not mutated` is the authoritative source for the 17 actual pass-shared test files.
## Baseline

Baseline coverage recorded over the full `tests/` tree with `python3 tools/replay_coverage_diff.py baseline --out .aw/state/trimcarry-8dyt88/base -- tests/`:

```
Baseline recorded to .aw/state/trimcarry-8dyt88/base/baseline.json
head: 86f0d88d9e967a79af6b3e6cd335081393fdbf72
xdist: True
functions: 4472
subprocess_only: 108
wall_s: 1209.56
```

- Baseline `head` equals measured HEAD `86f0d88d9e967a79af6b3e6cd335081393fdbf72`.
- Confirmed restored test modules from Orders 01 and 02 are present at HEAD:
  - `tests/test_benchmark_runners_reports.py` (Order 01, `24h56i`)
  - `tests/test_run_evidence_completion.py` (Order 02, `x9z0ve`)
## Method

The classification script `.aw/state/trimcarry-8dyt88/classify_keys.py` checks each key in `keys.txt` against HEAD source files using `extract_functions` from `tools.replay_coverage_diff` to detect `key-gone`, then checks `baseline.json` functions: absent keys are `unreached`, keys with `subprocess_only: true` are `subprocess-only`, and keys with test contexts are `reached`. Surviving test files are derived from the first two dot-segments of each test context (`tests.<module>[.<Class>].<test>` -> `tests/<module>.py`), verified to exist on disk at HEAD.

Selection limit:
The baseline excludes `slow` and `livecorpus` tests (`_build_pytest_cmd`), so `unreached` means "unreached by the routine non-slow selection", not "unreached by any test", and Orders 05 and 06 read it that way.

Script source:
```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Classify the 747 production keys against baseline.json and HEAD."""
import json
import re
import sys
from collections import Counter, defaultdict
from pathlib import Path

# Add tools to sys.path to import extract_functions
repo_root = Path.cwd().resolve()
sys.path.insert(0, str(repo_root))
from tools.replay_coverage_diff import extract_functions

scratch_dir = repo_root / ".aw/state/trimcarry-8dyt88"
keys_path = scratch_dir / "keys.txt"
hpjj44_path = scratch_dir / "hpjj44_rows.json"
baseline_path = scratch_dir / "base/baseline.json"

keys = [line.strip() for line in keys_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines() if line.strip()]
assert len(keys) == 747, f"Expected 747 keys, found {len(keys)}"

with open(hpjj44_path, encoding="utf-8") as f:
    hpjj44_data = json.load(f)
hpjj44_by_item = {r["item"]: r for r in hpjj44_data if r["type"] == "production-key"}

with open(baseline_path, encoding="utf-8") as f:
    baseline = json.load(f)
baseline_funcs = baseline.get("functions", {})

# Cache extract_functions per module
module_funcs_cache = {}

results = []
class_counts = Counter()
module_stats = defaultdict(lambda: {"keys": 0, "unreached": 0})
unmapped_contexts = []

for key in keys:
    hp_entry = hpjj44_by_item[key]
    reason = hp_entry["reason"]
    m = re.search(r"(\d+)\s+exclusive lines", hp_entry["details"])
    assert m, f"No exclusive lines found in {hp_entry['details']}"
    exclusive_lines = int(m.group(1))

    parts = key.split("::", 1)
    mod_path = parts[0]
    qualname = parts[1] if len(parts) > 1 else ""

    module_stats[mod_path]["keys"] += 1

    file_obj = repo_root / mod_path
    if not file_obj.is_file():
        reach_class = "key-gone"
        surviving_contexts_count = 0
        surviving_test_files = []
    else:
        if mod_path not in module_funcs_cache:
            try:
                src = file_obj.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
                spans = extract_functions(src, mod_path)
                module_funcs_cache[mod_path] = {s.qualname for s in spans}
            except Exception:
                module_funcs_cache[mod_path] = set()
        
        if qualname not in module_funcs_cache[mod_path]:
            reach_class = "key-gone"
            surviving_contexts_count = 0
            surviving_test_files = []
        else:
            # Function exists at HEAD. Check baseline
            if key not in baseline_funcs:
                reach_class = "unreached"
                surviving_contexts_count = 0
                surviving_test_files = []
                module_stats[mod_path]["unreached"] += 1
            elif baseline_funcs[key].get("subprocess_only"):
                reach_class = "subprocess-only"
                surviving_contexts_count = 0
                surviving_test_files = []
            else:
                reach_class = "reached"
                ctxs = baseline_funcs[key].get("contexts", [])
                surviving_contexts_count = len(ctxs)
                test_files_set = set()
                for ctx in ctxs:
                    # Derived from FIRST TWO dot-segments: tests.<module>[.<Class>].<test> -> tests/<module>.py
                    dot_parts = ctx.split(".")
                    if len(dot_parts) >= 2 and dot_parts[0] == "tests":
                        test_file_rel = f"tests/{dot_parts[1]}.py"
                        if (repo_root / test_file_rel).is_file():
                            test_files_set.add(test_file_rel)
                        else:
                            unmapped_contexts.append((ctx, f"derived file {test_file_rel} not found"))
                    else:
                        unmapped_contexts.append((ctx, "does not start with tests."))
                surviving_test_files = sorted(test_files_set)

    class_counts[reach_class] += 1
    results.append({
        "key": key,
        "reason": reason,
        "exclusive_lines": exclusive_lines,
        "reach_class": reach_class,
        "surviving_contexts_count": surviving_contexts_count,
        "surviving_test_files": surviving_test_files,
    })

# Write classified json
with open(scratch_dir / "classified_keys.json", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    json.dump({
        "class_counts": dict(class_counts),
        "unmapped_contexts": unmapped_contexts,
        "module_stats": dict(module_stats),
        "results": results,
    }, f, indent=2)

print(f"Total keys classified: {len(results)}")
print("Class counts:")
for cls in ["reached", "subprocess-only", "unreached", "key-gone"]:
    print(f"  {cls}: {class_counts[cls]}")
print(f"Sum: {sum(class_counts.values())}")
print(f"Unmapped contexts count: {len(unmapped_contexts)}")
```

## Pass-shared re-diff

Re-diff of the 17 pass-shared test files deleted in `19313eed` against the HEAD baseline.
Classification rule (first matching):
1. Replay verdict not `pass`/`fail` or `tests_count` == 0 -> `replay-unverified` (the replay did not run the deleted tests, so 0 exclusive lines proves nothing; `deleted_test_replay.py` verdicts `error`, `error-import`, `error-collect`, `no-tests`, `timeout`).
2. Exclusive line total > 0 -> `exclusive-now`.
3. Else -> `shared-confirmed`.

Order 06 (`z2xs98`) must carry a `replay-unverified` file as a gap exactly like `exclusive-now`.
